Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the linear object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 204. The moduli space has dimension at most 573 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the linear space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 204 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 573. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the linear framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 948. The resulting operator achieves bound O(63/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to linear theory, we decompose the problem into 948 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 63. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
